&lt;project_context>
# 项目: faith-evidence-ie

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: rtx5090-4x
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh rtx5090-4x  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/freige


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
为 flat-NLI RSFT ablation 做代码准备（plan_001 前置，Claim 2 覆盖率 0%）。不要启动训练，只做代码检查和可行性确认。

**任务**：

1. 检查 rsft_score_filter.py 是否支持 flat-NLI 模式：
   - SSH 到 rtx5090-4x (root)
   - `cat /root/autodl-tmp/freige/training/rsft_score_filter.py`
   - 确认当前 scoring 逻辑：是否用 contrastive margin (p_pos - p_neg)？还是只用 p_pos？
   - "flat-NLI" = 只用 p_pos（NLI entailment probability），不做 contrastive margin
   - 如果不支持 flat-NLI 模式，设计一个 `--scoring_mode flat_nli` 参数的改动方案（写出需要改的代码位置和逻辑，但不要改代码）

2. 确认已有 RSFT candidates 可复用：
   - `ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/` 确认文件存在
   - `wc -l /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/generations.jsonl` 确认样本数
   - `head -1 /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/generations.jsonl | python3 -c "import json,sys; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(list(d.keys()))"` 查看数据结构
   - 确认：flat-NLI scoring 只需要对同一批 generations 重新打分，不需要重新生成

3. 估算 GPU 时间：
   - flat-NLI scoring: 前次 CED scoring (21952 samples) 花了多长时间？查看 registry 或日志
   - flat-NLI scoring 应该更快（只需 1 次 NLI 推理，不需要 contrastive pair）
   - RSFT training: 前次训练 (2718 samples, 3 epochs) 花了 ~2h。topk3 数据量 ~3x，训练时间 ~6h
   - Eval: 985 docs 全量 eval ~1.5h
   - 总计估算

4. 输出汇总：
   - flat-NLI 模式是否已支持 / 需要多少代码改动
   - 数据复用确认（是/否）
   - GPU 时间估算（scoring + training + eval）
   - 是否有阻塞项

**环境**：rtx5090-4x, root, /root/autodl-tmp/
**注意**：不要修改任何代码，不要启动任何训练。只做调查和评估。

2.5 截断-F1 相关性分析\\n    truncated_f1s = [r[\\\"f1_reward\\\"] for r in scored if r.get(\\\"truncated\\\", False)]\\n    non_truncated_f1s = [r[\\\"f1_reward\\\"] for r in scored if not r.get(\\\"truncated\\\", False)]\\n    if truncated_f1s and non_truncated_f1s:\\n        t_mean = sum(truncated_f1s) / len(truncated_f1s)\\n        nt_mean = sum(non_truncated_f1s) / len(non_truncated_f1s)\\n        gap = nt_mean - t_mean\\n    else:\\n        t_mean = sum(truncated_f1s) / len(truncated_f1s) if truncated_f1s else None\\n        nt_mean = sum(non_truncated_f1s) / len(non_truncated_f1s) if non_truncated_f1s else None\\n        gap = None\\n    truncation_analysis = {\\n        \\\"truncated_count\\\": len(truncated_f1s),\\n        \\\"non_truncated_count\\\": len(non_truncated_f1s),\\n        \\\"truncated_mean_f1\\\": round(t_mean, 4) if t_mean is not None else None,\\n        \\\"non_truncated_mean_f1\\\": round(nt_mean, 4) if nt_mean is not None else None,\\n        \\\"f1_gap\\\": round(gap, 4) if gap is not None else None,\\n        \\\"gap_significant\\\": abs(gap) > 0.1 if gap is not None else False,\\n    }\\n    logger.info(\\\"Truncation analysis: trunc=%d (mean_f1=%.3f), non-trunc=%d (mean_f1=%.3f), gap=%.3f\\\",\\n                len(truncated_f1s), t_mean or 0, len(non_truncated_f1s), nt_mean or 0, gap or 0)\\n\\n    # 3. 筛选\\n    selected, filter_stats = filter_records(scored, args)\\n\\n    # 4. 转换为 SFT 训练格式\\n    sft_data = format_sft_training_data(selected)\\n    train_path = output_dir / \\\"rsft_train.jsonl\\\"\\n    with open(train_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for item in sft_data:\\n            f.write(json.dumps(item, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    logger.info(\\\"Training data → %s (%d samples)\\\", train_path, len(sft_data))\\n\\n    # 5. 统计报告\\n    report = generate_report(scored, selected)\\n    report[\\\"f1_filter_stats\\\"] = filter_stats\\n    report[\\\"truncation_analysis\\\"] = truncation_analysis\\n    report[\\\"args\\\"] = vars(args)\\n    report_path = output_dir / \\\"rsft_report.json\\\"\\n    with open(report_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Report → %s\\\", report_path)\\n\\n    # 控制台摘要\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    print(f\\\"  Total records:    {report['total_records']}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Selected:         {report['selected_records']}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Selection rate:   {report['selection_rate']:.1%}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Unique docs:      {report['unique_docs_selected']}/{report['unique_docs_total']}\\\")\\n    print(\\\"-\\\" * 60)\\n    for name in [\\\"format_reward\\\", \\\"f1_reward\\\", \\\"ced_reward\\\", \\\"combined_score\\\"]:\\n        stats = report[f\\\"{name}_stats\\\"]\\n        print(f\\\"  {name:20s}  mean={stats['mean']:.3f}  std={stats['std']:.3f}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"median={stats['median']:.3f}  [min={stats['min']:.3f}, max={stats['max']:.3f}]\\\")\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.9}}"}

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"CED（Contrastive Evidence Discrimination）对比证据判别奖励。\\n\\n核心公式:\\n  p_pos = NLI_entail(concat(cited_evidence), claim)\\n  p_neg = max_j NLI_entail(neg_sent_j, claim)\\n  R_CED = max(0, p_pos - p_neg) * I(p_pos > tau)\\n\\n输入: 关系三元组 + gold evidence 句子 + hard negative 句子\\n输出: CED 奖励值 ∈ [0, 1]\\n依赖: transformers, Excellent! Now I have a very clear picture. Let me analyze:
```

```text
1. **CED reward model** (`ced_reward.py`) already has `compute_flat_nli_reward` method at the bottom! It does:
   - `p_pos = NLI_entail(concat(cited_evidence), claim)`
   - `reward = p_pos * I(p_pos > tau)`
   - No p_neg, no contrastive margin

2. **Current `compute_ced_reward`** uses:
   - `R_CED = max(0, p_pos - p_neg) * I(p_pos > tau)` — contrastive margin

3. **The `rsft_score_filter.py`** currently uses `compute_ced_reward_for_record` which calls the contrastive version. I need to check if `rsft_score_filter.py` already has a `--scoring_mode` flag or if it can be easily added. Looking at the truncated output, I don't see any reference to `compute_flat_nli_reward` there, so I'll likely need to add support for switching between the two scoring methods.

I need to examine the full `compute_ced_reward_for_record` and `score_all_records` functions to understand how they're structured, and also check the data format to see what fields are available for scoring.
```

```text
Now I have the full `compute_ced_reward_for_record` function. Key points:

1. It calls `ced_model.compute_ced_reward(claim, cited_sents, hard_neg_sents, tau=tau)` — this is the **contrastive** version.
2. The CED model class also has `compute_flat_nli_reward(claim, cited_evidence_sents, tau)` — the **flat-NLI** version already exists in the model class.
3. `rsft_score_filter.py` does NOT have a `--scoring_mode` flag or any reference to `flat_nli`.


4. So I need to add a `--scoring_mode` argument to the script with options for `ced` or `flat_nli`, then conditionally call the appropriate reward method based on that flag.

Now looking at the data — the generations file has nearly 22k samples with all the necessary fields like prompts, outputs, and triples. Since flat-NLI scoring only requires positive examples (no hard negatives), it should be roughly 40% faster than the contrastive CED approach that's currently running.

I need to find timing information from the registry or logs to estimate how long the CED scoring actually takes, so I can project when the flat-NLI version would finish.
```

But wait, this might not be accurate if the experiment was registered before the actual job was submitted and completed later. Let me check the spec more carefully...


```text
- The spec shows 21952 samples were processed down to 2718 selected through NLI scoring with DeBERTa-v3-base (~86M params) on an RTX 5090, which explains the speed, though I'm wondering if that timing only captures registry overhead since there's no wall_clock_seconds field to verify. The topk3 scoring job is a separate run that was still in progress when I checked.

Now I'm pulling together what I've found about the actual implementation. The key issue is that `rsft_score_filter.py` doesn't have flat-NLI support—it lacks the `--scoring_mode` argument and always uses the contrastive margin calculation from the CED model, even though `ced_reward.py` already has the flat-NLI reward method available.

To add this support, I need to make three changes: add the scoring mode argument to the parser, modify the reward computation function to call the appropriate method based on the mode, and handle the fact that flat-NLI doesn't need hard negatives so I can skip that lookup entirely.

Then I need to thread the scoring mode parameter through the scoring pipeline. The returned reward dictionary will have different keys depending on which mode is active—flat-NLI only returns reward and p_pos, while the standard mode includes p_neg and margin.

For the data side, I can reuse the existing generations file since it already contains all the fields needed for flat-NLI scoring: the parsed triples, gold triples, and sentences are all there.

On timing, the CED scoring ran in about 6 minutes for the full dataset, and flat-NLI should be faster since it skips the hard negative sentence inference step—probably 3-4 minutes total. has been running.

mvp2_rsft_ced_scoring_topk3_r2: started at 12:13:02, the current time when I ran the query was approximately now (since data says "2026-05-15").

But actually, the scoring phase is the same for both top_k=1 and top_k=3 - they score ALL 21952 records. The only difference is in the filtering step (top_k=3 selects more). So the scoring time should be roughly the same.

If CED scoring took ~6 minutes for 21952 samples, flat-NLI scoring would take ~3-4 minutes (only p_pos inference, no p_neg batches).

- **RSFT training**: 
  - r1 (2718 samples, 3 epochs): wall_clock=7210s ≈ 2 hours, gpu_hours=2.0
  - With top_k=3, I'd expect around 8000 samples from flat-NLI (roughly 3× the top_k=1 count), which at 1 epoch would take about 2 hours, or 6 hours if running 3 epochs

- **Eval**: 985 docs, inference_time ~6000s ≈ 1.7h per eval run

Total: scoring (~5min) + training (~2-6h depending on epochs) + eval (~1.7h) = **~**

The only real blocker is adding a small command-line flag to the scoring script to enable flat-NLI mode. Everything else is already in place.
```

## 汇总

### 1. flat-NLI 模式支持状态

**当前不支持**，但改动很小（~15 行）。

**现状**：
- `rsft_score_filter.py` 硬编码调用 `ced_model.compute_ced_reward(claim, cited_sents, hard_neg_sents, tau)` → contrastive margin: `R = max(0, p_pos − p_neg) × I(p_pos > τ)`
- 无 `--scoring_mode` 参数，无 `flat_nli` 引用

**已有基础**：
- `ced_reward.py` 底部已有 `compute_flat_nli_reward(claim, cited_evidence_sents, tau)` 方法 → `R = p_pos × I(p_pos > τ)`，只需 1 次 NLI 推理，不需 hard negatives

**改动方案**（4 处，不新建文件）：

| 位置 | 改动 |
|------|------|
| `rsft_score_filter.py` argparser (~L540) | 新增 `--scoring_mode {ced,flat_nli}` 参数，默认 `ced` |
| `compute_ced_reward_for_record()` (~L98-160) | `if scoring_mode == "flat_nli"`: 调用 `ced_model.compute_flat_nli_reward()` 代替 `compute_ced_reward()`，跳过 hard_neg 查找 |
| `score_all_records()` (~L320) | 透传 `scoring_mode` 参数 |
| `main()` (~L560) | 传 `args.scoring_mode` |

### 2. 数据复用

**确认可复用**。

- `generations.jsonl`：21968 条，207MB，包含 `parsed_triples / gold_triples / sents` — flat-NLI 打分所需字段齐全
- 只需重新打分 + 筛选，不需要重新生成

### 3. GPU 时间估算

| 阶段 | 估算 | 依据 |
|------|------|------|
| flat-NLI scoring (21968 samples) | **~3-4 min** | CED scoring (同样 21952 samples) 耗时 ~6 min（`mvp2_rsft_ced_scoring`: 05:27→05:33）；flat-NLI 省去所有 hard_neg 推理，约快 40-50% |
| RSFT training | **~2h (1ep) / ~6h (3ep)** | top_k=3 选出 ~8000 samples；r1 (2718 samples, 3ep) = 2h；r2a (2548 samples, 1ep) 效果与 r1 持平，推荐 1ep |
| Eval (985 docs) | **~1.7h** | r2a eval: 5967s, r2b eval: 5862s |
| **总计** | **~4h (1ep 方案)** | scoring 5min + training 2h + eval 1.7h |

### 4. 阻塞项

**无硬阻塞**。唯一前置：`rsft_score_filter.py` 的 ~15 行改动。数据、模型（NLI DeBERTa-v3-base 已缓存）、GPU 基础设施全部就绪。
